~~~java
package com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.service.task.impl;

import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.TestRuns;

import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.business.DocumentStructureBusiness;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.business.GetCompartimentRunsBusiness;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.config.EngineFormattingProperties;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.CompartmentRunSelection;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.DocumentDomain;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.Run;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.RunError;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.RunExecutionContext;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.RunProperties;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.RunResult;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.dynamic.report.DBlocInfo;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.element.TElement;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.helper.TElementHelper;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.project.QxpProject;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.task.TaskCompartiment;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.dto.RunIdDto;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.enums.BlocActionEnum;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.enums.StaticTElementNameEnum;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.integration.soap.generated.Box;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.integration.soap.generated.Geometry;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.integration.soap.generated.Layout;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.integration.soap.generated.Project;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.integration.soap.generated.Spread;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.service.ProcessRunService;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.test.util.ReflectionTestUtils;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNotEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertSame;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.any;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.eq;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.same;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.mock;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.never;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.verify;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.when;

class CompartimentTaskProcessStrategyTest {

    @Test
    void degradedChildProjectContributesNoBoxesWithoutAddingAnError() {
        DocumentStructureBusiness structures = mock(DocumentStructureBusiness.class);
        CompartimentTaskProcessStrategy strategy = strategy(
                mock(GetCompartimentRunsBusiness.class), mock(ProcessRunService.class), structures);

        Run parent = TestRuns.run();
        parent.setId(42);
        TaskCompartiment task = new TaskCompartiment(5, parent);

        Run child = TestRuns.run();
        child.setId(99);
        RunResult result = new RunResult();
        result.setFinalQxp(new DocumentDomain(700, "child", "QXP",
                DocumentDomain.FILE_DOCUMENT_FINAL_PREFIX, new byte[]{1}));
        child.setResult(result);

        when(structures.ensureProject(eq(parent), any(DocumentDomain.class)))
                .thenAnswer(invocation -> {
                    DocumentDomain snapshot = invocation.getArgument(1);
                    snapshot.setModeDegrade(true);
                    return QxpProject.empty();
                });

        Integer lastPage = ReflectionTestUtils.invokeMethod(strategy, "addRunBlocs", task, child, 7);

        assertEquals(7, lastPage);
        assertTrue(parent.getErrors().isEmpty());
    }

    @Test
    void preparationRetainsCursorOrderDuplicatesAndLegacyIntegerIdentifiers() {
        GetCompartimentRunsBusiness selections = mock(GetCompartimentRunsBusiness.class);
        CompartimentTaskProcessStrategy strategy = strategy(
                selections, mock(ProcessRunService.class), mock(DocumentStructureBusiness.class));
        TaskCompartiment task = taskWithMode(42, 5);
        when(selections.execute(any(Integer.class), any(), any(Integer.class), any(Integer.class),
                any(Integer.class), any(), eq(true))).thenReturn(List.of(
                new CompartmentRunSelection("A", 9),
                new CompartmentRunSelection("A", 0),
                new CompartmentRunSelection("B", -7),
                new CompartmentRunSelection("C", null)));

        ReflectionTestUtils.invokeMethod(strategy, "prepareRuns", task);

        assertEquals(List.of(9, 0, -7), task.getChildRuns().stream().map(Run::getId).toList());
        assertEquals(1, task.getRun().getErrors().size());
        assertEquals(RunError.UNSPECIFIED, task.getRun().getErrors().get(0).getCategory());
        assertSame(task.getRun().getExecutionContext(), task.getChildRuns().get(0).getExecutionContext());
    }

    @Test
    void emptyCursorAddsOneUnspecifiedParentError() {
        GetCompartimentRunsBusiness selections = mock(GetCompartimentRunsBusiness.class);
        CompartimentTaskProcessStrategy strategy = strategy(
                selections, mock(ProcessRunService.class), mock(DocumentStructureBusiness.class));
        TaskCompartiment task = taskWithMode(42, 2);
        when(selections.execute(any(Integer.class), any(), any(Integer.class), any(Integer.class),
                any(Integer.class), any(), eq(false))).thenReturn(List.of());

        ReflectionTestUtils.invokeMethod(strategy, "prepareRuns", task);

        assertTrue(task.getChildRuns().isEmpty());
        assertEquals(1, task.getRun().getErrors().size());
        assertEquals(RunError.UNSPECIFIED, task.getRun().getErrors().get(0).getCategory());
    }

    @Test
    void rawGenerateBitSelectsFullChildLifecycleAndExtraBitOnlySelectsPreviousLifecycle() {
        ProcessRunService processor = mock(ProcessRunService.class);
        CompartimentTaskProcessStrategy strategy = strategy(
                mock(GetCompartimentRunsBusiness.class), processor,
                mock(DocumentStructureBusiness.class));

        TaskCompartiment generate = taskWithMode(42, 5);
        Run generatedInput = TestRuns.run();
        generatedInput.setId(9);
        generate.getChildRuns().add(generatedInput);
        Run generatedOutput = TestRuns.run();
        generatedOutput.setId(9);
        generatedOutput.trace("generated child event");
        when(processor.runChildProcessor(any(RunIdDto.class),
                same(generate.getRun().getExecutionContext()))).thenReturn(generatedOutput);

        ReflectionTestUtils.invokeMethod(strategy, "executeRuns", generate);

        assertSame(generatedOutput, generate.getChildRuns().get(0));
        assertTrue(generate.getRun().getTraceLog().contains("childRunId=9"));
        assertTrue(generate.getRun().getTraceLog().contains("generated child event"));
        verify(processor).runChildProcessor(any(RunIdDto.class),
                same(generate.getRun().getExecutionContext()));

        TaskCompartiment previous = taskWithMode(43, 4);
        Run previousInput = TestRuns.run();
        previousInput.setId(-7);
        previous.getChildRuns().add(previousInput);
        Run previousOutput = TestRuns.run();
        previousOutput.setId(-7);
        previousOutput.trace("previous child event");
        when(processor.runPreviousChildProcessor(any(RunIdDto.class),
                same(previous.getRun().getExecutionContext()))).thenReturn(previousOutput);

        ReflectionTestUtils.invokeMethod(strategy, "executeRuns", previous);

        assertSame(previousOutput, previous.getChildRuns().get(0));
        assertTrue(previous.getRun().getTraceLog().contains("childRunId=-7"));
        assertTrue(previous.getRun().getTraceLog().contains("previous child event"));
        verify(processor).runPreviousChildProcessor(any(RunIdDto.class),
                same(previous.getRun().getExecutionContext()));
    }

    @Test
    void nonIncorporatingModeMarksTaskNotTodoWithoutCreatingModifications() {
        CompartimentTaskProcessStrategy strategy = strategy(
                mock(GetCompartimentRunsBusiness.class), mock(ProcessRunService.class),
                mock(DocumentStructureBusiness.class));
        TaskCompartiment task = taskWithMode(42, 5);
        task.setTodo(true);

        ReflectionTestUtils.invokeMethod(strategy, "renderRuns", task);

        assertFalse(task.isTodo());
        assertTrue(task.getBlocsModify().isEmpty());
    }

    @Test
    void renameMatchesLegacyEmptyWhitespaceDefaultTruncationAndNullFormattingRules() {
        CompartimentTaskProcessStrategy strategy = strategy(
                mock(GetCompartimentRunsBusiness.class), mock(ProcessRunService.class),
                mock(DocumentStructureBusiness.class));

        assertEquals("NAME_FUND", rename(strategy, box("NAME", "8", "1", "false"), "FUND"));
        assertEquals("NAME_FUND", rename(strategy, box("NAME_FUND", "8", "1", "false"), "FUND"));
        assertEquals("ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWX_FUND",
                rename(strategy, box("ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ", "8", "1", "false"), "FUND"));
        assertEquals("   _FUND", rename(strategy, box("   ", "8", "1", "false"), "FUND"));
        assertEquals("NAME_", rename(strategy, box("NAME", "8", "1", "false"), null));

        String emptyGenerated = rename(strategy, box("", "8", "1", "false"), "FUND");
        String defaultGenerated = rename(strategy, box("Box8", "8", "1", "false"), "FUND");
        String nullUidGenerated = rename(strategy, box("Box", null, "1", "false"), "FUND");
        assertNotEquals("", emptyGenerated);
        assertNotEquals("Box8", defaultGenerated);
        assertNotEquals("Box", nullUidGenerated);
    }

    @Test
    void malformedProjectNodesAndDuplicateFinalNamesFailInsteadOfBeingSilentlySkipped() {
        CompartimentTaskProcessStrategy strategy = strategy(
                mock(GetCompartimentRunsBusiness.class), mock(ProcessRunService.class),
                mock(DocumentStructureBusiness.class));
        TaskCompartiment task = taskWithMode(42, 2);
        Run child = child("FUND");

        assertThrows(NullPointerException.class,
                () -> ReflectionTestUtils.invokeMethod(strategy, "addBlocs", task,
                        new QxpProject(null), child, 0));

        Project nullLayout = new Project();
        nullLayout.setLayouts(new Layout[]{null});
        assertThrows(NullPointerException.class,
                () -> ReflectionTestUtils.invokeMethod(strategy, "addBlocs", task,
                        new QxpProject(nullLayout), child, 0));

        Project duplicates = project(
                box("SAME", "1", "1", "false"),
                box("SAME", "2", "1", "false"));
        assertThrows(IllegalStateException.class,
                () -> ReflectionTestUtils.invokeMethod(strategy, "addBlocs", task,
                        new QxpProject(duplicates), child, 0));
    }

    @Test
    void pageSelectionUsesInvariantConversionPastboardFilterAndLiteralVisibility() {
        CompartimentTaskProcessStrategy strategy = strategy(
                mock(GetCompartimentRunsBusiness.class), mock(ProcessRunService.class),
                mock(DocumentStructureBusiness.class));
        TaskCompartiment task = taskWithMode(42, 2);
        Run child = child("FUND");
        Project project = project(
                box("VISIBLE", "1", "2.9", "false"),
                box("SUPPRESSED", "2", "2.9", "true"),
                box("PASTBOARD", "3", "3*", "false"),
                box("INVALID", "4", "not-a-page", "false"));

        Integer lastPage = ReflectionTestUtils.invokeMethod(strategy, "addBlocs", task,
                new QxpProject(project), child, 0);

        assertEquals(2, lastPage);
        assertTrue(task.getBlocsModify().containsKey("INVALID_FUND"));
        assertTrue(task.getBlocsModify().containsKey("VISIBLE_FUND"));
        assertTrue(task.getBlocsModify().containsKey("SUPPRESSED_FUND"));
        assertFalse(task.getBlocsModify().containsKey("PASTBOARD_FUND"));
    }

    @Test
    void allSuppressedChildPageContributesNoPageOrBox() {
        CompartimentTaskProcessStrategy strategy = strategy(
                mock(GetCompartimentRunsBusiness.class), mock(ProcessRunService.class),
                mock(DocumentStructureBusiness.class));
        TaskCompartiment task = taskWithMode(42, 2);

        Integer lastPage = ReflectionTestUtils.invokeMethod(strategy, "addBlocs", task,
                new QxpProject(project(
                        box("ANCHOR", "1", "1", "true"),
                        box("UNSET", "2", "1", null))), child("FUND"), 7);

        assertEquals(7, lastPage);
        assertTrue(task.getBlocsModify().isEmpty());
    }

    @Test
    void failedChildDoesNotPreventLaterUsableChildAndOldPageRemovalIsInclusive() {
        DocumentStructureBusiness structures = mock(DocumentStructureBusiness.class);
        CompartimentTaskProcessStrategy strategy = strategy(
                mock(GetCompartimentRunsBusiness.class), mock(ProcessRunService.class), structures);
        TaskCompartiment task = taskWithMode(42, 2);
        task.setStartAnchor(anchor("START", "START_UID", 3));
        task.setEndAnchor(anchor("END", "END_UID", 5));

        Run failed = child("FAIL");
        Run usable = child("FUND");
        RunResult usableResult = new RunResult();
        usableResult.setFinalQxp(new DocumentDomain(701, "usable", "QXP",
                DocumentDomain.FILE_DOCUMENT_FINAL_PREFIX, new byte[]{1}));
        usable.setResult(usableResult);
        task.getChildRuns().add(failed);
        task.getChildRuns().add(usable);
        when(structures.ensureProject(eq(task.getRun()), any(DocumentDomain.class)))
                .thenReturn(new QxpProject(project(box("VISIBLE", "1", "4", "false"))));

        ReflectionTestUtils.invokeMethod(strategy, "renderRuns", task);

        assertEquals(1, task.getRun().getErrors().size());
        assertEquals(RunError.UNSPECIFIED, task.getRun().getErrors().get(0).getCategory());
        assertTrue(task.getBlocsModify().containsKey("VISIBLE_FUND"));
        assertEquals(BlocActionEnum.CREATE, task.getBlocsModify().get("T5_P0").getAction());
        assertEquals(BlocActionEnum.REMOVE, task.getBlocsModify().get("T5_P1").getAction());
        assertEquals(BlocActionEnum.REMOVE, task.getBlocsModify().get("T5_P2").getAction());
        assertEquals(BlocActionEnum.REMOVE, task.getBlocsModify().get("T5_P3").getAction());
        assertTrue(task.getBlocsModify().get("START").isPagination());
        assertTrue(task.getBlocsModify().get("END").isPagination());
    }

    @Test
    void missingMoveTemplateFailsTheCompartmentTaskPath() {
        CompartimentTaskProcessStrategy strategy = strategy(
                mock(GetCompartimentRunsBusiness.class), mock(ProcessRunService.class),
                mock(DocumentStructureBusiness.class));
        TaskCompartiment task = taskWithMode(42, 2);
        task.setStartAnchor(anchor("START", "START_UID", 3));
        task.setEndAnchor(anchor("END", "END_UID", 3));

        @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
        Map<StaticTElementNameEnum, TElement> staticElements =
                (Map<StaticTElementNameEnum, TElement>) ReflectionTestUtils.getField(
                        TElementHelper.class, "STATIC_ELEMENTS");
        TElement moveTemplate = staticElements.remove(StaticTElementNameEnum.MOVE_BLOC);
        try {
            assertThrows(NullPointerException.class,
                    () -> ReflectionTestUtils.invokeMethod(strategy, "renderRuns", task));
        } finally {
            staticElements.put(StaticTElementNameEnum.MOVE_BLOC, moveTemplate);
        }
    }

    @Test
    void everyDirectlyDereferencedMalformedDomNodeFails() {
        CompartimentTaskProcessStrategy strategy = strategy(
                mock(GetCompartimentRunsBusiness.class), mock(ProcessRunService.class),
                mock(DocumentStructureBusiness.class));
        Run child = child("FUND");

        Layout nullSpreadLayout = new Layout();
        nullSpreadLayout.setSpreads(new Spread[]{null});
        Project nullSpreadProject = new Project();
        nullSpreadProject.setLayouts(new Layout[]{nullSpreadLayout});
        assertMalformed(strategy, taskWithMode(42, 2), child, nullSpreadProject);

        Spread nullBoxSpread = new Spread();
        nullBoxSpread.setBoxes(new Box[]{null});
        Layout nullBoxLayout = new Layout();
        nullBoxLayout.setSpreads(new Spread[]{nullBoxSpread});
        Project nullBoxProject = new Project();
        nullBoxProject.setLayouts(new Layout[]{nullBoxLayout});
        assertMalformed(strategy, taskWithMode(43, 2), child, nullBoxProject);

        Box noGeometry = new Box();
        noGeometry.setName("NO_GEOMETRY");
        assertMalformed(strategy, taskWithMode(44, 2), child, project(noGeometry));

        Box noPage = box("NO_PAGE", "1", null, "false");
        assertMalformed(strategy, taskWithMode(45, 2), child, project(noPage));
    }

    @Test
    void overflowingPageIdentifierFailsInsteadOfWrappingOrDroppingTheBox() {
        CompartimentTaskProcessStrategy strategy = strategy(
                mock(GetCompartimentRunsBusiness.class), mock(ProcessRunService.class),
                mock(DocumentStructureBusiness.class));

        assertThrows(ArithmeticException.class,
                () -> ReflectionTestUtils.invokeMethod(strategy, "addBlocs",
                        taskWithMode(42, 2),
                        new QxpProject(project(box("OVERFLOW", "1", "2147483648", "false"))),
                        child("FUND"), 0));
    }

    private static CompartimentTaskProcessStrategy strategy(
            GetCompartimentRunsBusiness selections, ProcessRunService processor,
            DocumentStructureBusiness structures) {
        EngineFormattingProperties formatting = new EngineFormattingProperties();
        formatting.setDecimalSeparator(".");
        com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.service.RunFactory runFactory =
                mock(com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.service.RunFactory.class);
        when(runFactory.create()).thenAnswer(invocation -> TestRuns.run());
        return new CompartimentTaskProcessStrategy(
                selections, processor, structures, formatting, runFactory);
    }

    private static TaskCompartiment taskWithMode(int runId, int mode) {
        Run parent = TestRuns.run();
        parent.setId(runId);
        parent.setExecutionContext(new RunExecutionContext(runId));
        RunProperties properties = new RunProperties();
        properties.setCompartimentModeCode(mode);
        properties.setIdGabarit(10);
        properties.setIdFndCode("PARENT");
        properties.setIdLangue(1);
        parent.setRunProperties(properties);
        TaskCompartiment task = new TaskCompartiment(5, parent);
        task.setIdGabaritFils(20);
        return task;
    }

    private static Run child(String fundCode) {
        Run child = TestRuns.run();
        child.setId(99);
        RunProperties properties = new RunProperties();
        properties.setIdFndCode(fundCode);
        child.setRunProperties(properties);
        return child;
    }

    private static DBlocInfo anchor(String name, String uid, int page) {
        DBlocInfo anchor = new DBlocInfo();
        anchor.setName(name);
        anchor.setUid(uid);
        anchor.setPage(page);
        return anchor;
    }

    private static void assertMalformed(CompartimentTaskProcessStrategy strategy,
                                        TaskCompartiment task, Run child, Project project) {
        assertThrows(NullPointerException.class,
                () -> ReflectionTestUtils.invokeMethod(strategy, "addBlocs", task,
                        new QxpProject(project), child, 0));
    }

    private static Project project(Box... boxes) {
        Spread spread = new Spread();
        spread.setBoxes(boxes);
        Layout layout = new Layout();
        layout.setSpreads(new Spread[]{spread});
        Project project = new Project();
        project.setLayouts(new Layout[]{layout});
        return project;
    }

    private static Box box(String name, String uid, String page, String suppressOutput) {
        Geometry geometry = new Geometry();
        geometry.setPage(page);
        geometry.setSuppressOutput(suppressOutput);
        Box box = new Box();
        box.setName(name);
        box.setUID(uid);
        box.setGeometry(geometry);
        return box;
    }

    private static String rename(CompartimentTaskProcessStrategy strategy, Box box, String suffix) {
        return ReflectionTestUtils.invokeMethod(strategy, "renameBloc", box, suffix);
    }
}
~~~

### [83] `src/test/java/com/socgen/sgs/api/quark/engine/service/task/impl/DidTaskPostProcessStrategyTest.java`

SHA-256: `05a163ae38db1efe1551df819479f072f89ba86e0919160a3832bdffefa3d962`

~~~java
package com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.service.task.impl;

import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.TestRuns;

import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.DocumentDomain;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.config.EngineFormattingProperties;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.Run;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.RunError;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.RunProperties;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.bloc.BlocBox;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.dynamic.report.DBlocInfo;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.helper.DataTypeHelper;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.task.TaskBase;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.task.TaskDid;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.task.TaskSql;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.xml.QxpXml;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.math.BigDecimal;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.mock;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.never;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.verify;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.when;

class DidTaskPostProcessStrategyTest {

    private SystemTaskProcessStrategy systemTaskProcessStrategy;
    private DidTaskPostProcessStrategy strategy;
    private Run run;
    private TaskDid didTask;
    private QxpXml qxpXml;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        systemTaskProcessStrategy = mock(SystemTaskProcessStrategy.class);
        EngineFormattingProperties formatting = new EngineFormattingProperties();
        formatting.setDatePattern("dd/MM/yyyy");
        formatting.setDateTimePattern("dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm:ss");
        formatting.setDecimalSeparator(",");
        formatting.setGroupSeparator(" ");
        formatting.setCurrencySymbol("EUR");
        strategy = new DidTaskPostProcessStrategy(systemTaskProcessStrategy, new DataTypeHelper(formatting));
        qxpXml = mock(QxpXml.class);

        run = TestRuns.run();
        run.setId(100);
        run.setRunProperties(new RunProperties());
        DocumentDomain document = new DocumentDomain();
        document.setQxpXml(qxpXml);
        run.setGabarit(document);
        didTask = new TaskDid(TaskDid.DID_TASK_ID, run);
        run.getTasks().put(didTask.getId(), didTask);
    }

    @Test
    void missingDidInValueBranchAddsExactUnspecifiedErrorAndReturns() {
        when(qxpXml.getUID("DID")).thenReturn("");

        strategy.postProcess(didTask);

        assertEquals(1, run.getErrors().size());
        assertEquals(RunError.UNSPECIFIED, run.getErrors().get(0).getCategory());
        assertEquals("The DID block is missing from the document", run.getErrors().get(0).getMessage());
        verify(systemTaskProcessStrategy, never()).process(didTask);
    }

    @Test
    void whitespaceUidIsConfiguredTextAndContinuesValueProcessing() {
        when(qxpXml.getUID("DID")).thenReturn(" ");

        strategy.postProcess(didTask);

        assertEquals(0, run.getErrors().size());
        verify(systemTaskProcessStrategy).process(didTask);
    }

    @Test
    void missingDidInMoveBranchAddsExactUnspecifiedErrorAndReturns() {
        TaskSql paginationTask = new TaskSql(10, run);
        BlocBox paginationBloc = new BlocBox(paginationTask, "PAGE", "value");
        paginationBloc.setPagination(true);
        paginationTask.getBlocsModify().put(paginationBloc.getName(), paginationBloc);
        run.getTasks().put(paginationTask.getId(), paginationTask);
        when(qxpXml.getBlocInfo("DID")).thenReturn(null);

        strategy.postProcess(didTask);

        assertEquals(1, run.getErrors().size());
        assertEquals(RunError.UNSPECIFIED, run.getErrors().get(0).getCategory());
        assertEquals("The DID block is missing from the document", run.getErrors().get(0).getMessage());
        verify(systemTaskProcessStrategy, never()).process(didTask);
    }

    @Test
    void moveCoordinatesUseConfiguredDecimalSeparator() {
        TaskSql paginationTask = paginationTask();
        run.getTasks().put(paginationTask.getId(), paginationTask);
        DBlocInfo didInfo = new DBlocInfo();
        didInfo.setUid("did-uid");
        didInfo.setLeft(new BigDecimal("14.8931"));
        didInfo.setTop(new BigDecimal("2.50"));
        didInfo.setRight(new BigDecimal("20"));
        didInfo.setBottom(new BigDecimal("30.125"));
        when(qxpXml.getBlocInfo("DID")).thenReturn(didInfo);

        strategy.postProcess(didTask);

        assertEquals("14,8931", didTask.getTBoxSrcBox().getGeometry().getPosition().getLeft());
        assertEquals("2,50", didTask.getTBoxSrcBox().getGeometry().getPosition().getTop());
        assertEquals("20", didTask.getTBoxSrcBox().getGeometry().getPosition().getRight());
        assertEquals("30,125", didTask.getTBoxSrcBox().getGeometry().getPosition().getBottom());
        verify(systemTaskProcessStrategy).process(didTask);
    }

    @Test
    void paginationBreakStopsOnlyCurrentInnerLoop() {
        TaskSql paginationTask = paginationTask();
        run.getTasks().put(paginationTask.getId(), paginationTask);
        TaskBase laterTask = mock(TaskBase.class);
        when(laterTask.getBlocsModify()).thenThrow(new IllegalStateException("later task visited"));
        run.getTasks().put(99, laterTask);

        IllegalStateException error = assertThrows(IllegalStateException.class,
                () -> strategy.postProcess(didTask));

        assertEquals("later task visited", error.getMessage());
    }

    private TaskSql paginationTask() {
        TaskSql paginationTask = new TaskSql(10, run);
        BlocBox paginationBloc = new BlocBox(paginationTask, "PAGE", "value");
        paginationBloc.setPagination(true);
        paginationTask.getBlocsModify().put(paginationBloc.getName(), paginationBloc);
        return paginationTask;
    }
}
~~~

### [84] `src/test/java/com/socgen/sgs/api/quark/engine/service/task/impl/DocumentTaskProcessStrategyTest.java`

SHA-256: `ce3a323c9aaa963582cbf28e7b774656c4d6fe22875a2d5e7231c55e8a27056b`

~~~java
package com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.service.task.impl;

import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.TestRuns;

import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.business.DocumentStructureBusiness;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.DocumentDomain;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.Run;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.RunProperties;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.bloc.BlocBox;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.bloc.BlocTable;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.element.TBox;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.element.TElement;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.element.TTable;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.project.QxpProject;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.task.TaskDocument;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.xml.QxpXml;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.enums.SubTaskTypeEnum;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.integration.soap.generated.Box;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.integration.soap.generated.Table;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertSame;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.mock;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.when;

class DocumentTaskProcessStrategyTest {

    private DocumentStructureBusiness structureBusiness;
    private DocumentTaskProcessStrategy strategy;
    private Run run;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        structureBusiness = mock(DocumentStructureBusiness.class);
        strategy = new DocumentTaskProcessStrategy(structureBusiness);
        run = TestRuns.run();
        run.setId(42);
        run.setRunProperties(new RunProperties());
        DocumentDomain gabarit = new DocumentDomain();
        gabarit.setQxpXml(mock(QxpXml.class));
        run.setGabarit(gabarit);
    }

    @Test
    void missingDestinationAddsUnspecifiedErrorWithoutCreatingBlocs() {
        TaskDocument task = task(SubTaskTypeEnum.FILE_IMG);
        task.setDestinationBlocName("");

        strategy.process(task);

        assertEquals(1, run.getErrors().size());
        assertEquals(1, run.getErrors().get(0).getCategory());
        assertEquals(0, task.getBlocsModify().size());
    }

    @Test
    void missingRequiredImageDocumentFailsInsteadOfSilentlySkipping() {
        TaskDocument task = task(SubTaskTypeEnum.FILE_IMG);
        task.setDestinationBlocName("LOGO");

        assertThrows(NullPointerException.class, () -> strategy.process(task));
    }

    @Test
    void qxpValueCopyPreservesWhitespaceAndTrailingEmptyTokens() {
        QxpXml xml = mock(QxpXml.class);
        when(xml.getValue(" SRC ")).thenReturn("first");
        when(xml.getValue("")).thenReturn("second");
        DocumentDomain source = new DocumentDomain();
        source.setQxpXml(xml);
        TaskDocument task = task(SubTaskTypeEnum.FILE_QXP_DATA);
        task.setDocument(source);
        task.setSourceBlocName(" SRC |");
        task.setDestinationBlocName(" DEST |");

        strategy.process(task);

        assertEquals(List.of(" DEST ", ""), List.copyOf(task.getBlocsUpdate().keySet()));
        assertEquals("first", ((BlocBox) task.getBlocsUpdate().get(" DEST ")).getValue());
        assertEquals("second", ((BlocBox) task.getBlocsUpdate().get("")).getValue());
    }

    @Test
    void qxpShortDestinationFailsAtTheAcceptedTaskBoundary() {
        DocumentDomain source = new DocumentDomain();
        source.setQxpXml(mock(QxpXml.class));
        TaskDocument task = task(SubTaskTypeEnum.FILE_QXP_DATA);
        task.setDocument(source);
        task.setSourceBlocName("A|B");
        task.setDestinationBlocName("X");

        assertThrows(ArrayIndexOutOfBoundsException.class, () -> strategy.process(task));
    }

    @Test
    void docEosStyleUsesRawSourceBoxAndPreservesAcceptedTBoxDuplicateError() {
        Box sourceBox = new Box();
        Box sourceExtra = new Box();
        TBox sourceElement = new TBox(sourceBox, sourceExtra);
        TaskDocument task = styleTask(Map.of("SRC", sourceElement));

        strategy.process(task);

        BlocBox bloc = (BlocBox) task.getBlocsModify().get("DEST");
        assertSame(sourceBox, bloc.getSrcBox());
        assertSame(sourceExtra, bloc.getSrcExtraBox());
        assertEquals(1, run.getErrors().size());
        assertEquals(1, run.getErrors().get(0).getCategory());
    }

    @Test
    void docEosStyleUsesRawSourceTableWithoutTheTBoxQuirk() {
        Table sourceTable = new Table();
        TTable sourceElement = new TTable(sourceTable);
        TaskDocument task = styleTask(Map.of("SRC", sourceElement));

        strategy.process(task);

        BlocTable bloc = (BlocTable) task.getBlocsModify().get("DEST");
        assertSame(sourceTable, bloc.getSrcTable());
        assertEquals(0, run.getErrors().size());
    }

    @Test
    void pdfWritesEmptyOffsetAndPreservesTrailingPositionToken() {
        QxpXml gabaritXml = run.getGabarit().getQxpXml();
        when(gabaritXml.getListBoxNameStartWith("PDF")).thenReturn(List.of("PDF_1"));
        DocumentDomain source = new DocumentDomain();
        source.setPdfFiles(List.of("page-1.pdf", "page-2.pdf"));
        TaskDocument task = task(SubTaskTypeEnum.FILE_PDF);
        task.setDestinationBlocName("PDF");
        task.setDocument(source);
        task.setOffsetValues(null);
        task.setPositionValues("1;2;3;");

        strategy.process(task);

        BlocBox first = (BlocBox) task.getBlocsModify().get("PDF_1");
        BlocBox second = (BlocBox) task.getBlocsModify().get("PDF_2");
        assertEquals("", first.getSrcExtraBox().getPicture().getOffsetDown());
        assertEquals("", second.getSrcExtraBox().getPicture().getOffsetDown());
        assertEquals("1", second.getSrcBox().getGeometry().getPosition().getLeft());
        assertEquals("", second.getSrcBox().getGeometry().getPosition().getBottom());
    }

    private TaskDocument task(SubTaskTypeEnum type) {
        TaskDocument task = new TaskDocument(100, run);
        task.setSubTaskType(type);
        task.setTodo(true);
        return task;
    }

    private TaskDocument styleTask(Map<String, TElement> elements) {
        QxpProject project = mock(QxpProject.class);
        when(project.getElements()).thenReturn(new LinkedHashMap<>(elements));
        DocumentDomain source = new DocumentDomain();
        source.setQxpProject(project);
        TaskDocument task = task(SubTaskTypeEnum.FILE_QXP_DATA);
        task.setDocument(source);
        task.setSourceBlocName("SRC");
        task.setDestinationBlocName("DEST");
        task.setConserverStyle(true);
        return task;
    }
}
~~~

### [85] `src/test/java/com/socgen/sgs/api/quark/engine/service/task/impl/DynamiqueTaskProcessStrategyTest.java`

SHA-256: `7bcd0629e5ed23b4cfb2111e77bc1a77dd68eeefcad937dd2dac822aa3d8c2c2`

~~~java
package com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.service.task.impl;

import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.TestRuns;

import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.business.DocumentStructureBusiness;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.config.EngineFormattingProperties;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.DocumentDomain;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.Run;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.RunError;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.RunProperties;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.bloc.BlocPage;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.dynamic.report.DBlocInfo;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.exception.EngineException;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.port.DynamicQueryPort;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.task.TaskDynamique;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertInstanceOf;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.anyMap;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.eq;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.mock;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.never;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.verify;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.verifyNoInteractions;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.when;

class DynamiqueTaskProcessStrategyTest {

    private DynamicQueryPort queryPort;
    private DocumentStructureBusiness structures;
    private DynamiqueTaskProcessStrategy strategy;
    private Run run;
    private TaskDynamique task;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        queryPort = mock(DynamicQueryPort.class);
        structures = mock(DocumentStructureBusiness.class);
        EngineFormattingProperties formatting = new EngineFormattingProperties();
        formatting.setDecimalSeparator(".");
        strategy = new DynamiqueTaskProcessStrategy(queryPort, structures, formatting);

        run = TestRuns.run();
        run.setId(900);
        run.setRunProperties(new RunProperties());
        run.setGabaritTemplate(new DocumentDomain());
        task = new TaskDynamique(40, run);
        task.setSql("SELECT dynamic data");
        task.setDestinationBlocName("REPORT");
        task.setStartAnchor(anchor("START_UID", "REPORT_START", 2, "1", "1", "2", "2"));
        task.setEndAnchor(anchor("END_UID", "REPORT_END", 2, "10", "10", "11", "11"));
    }

    @Test
    void missingGabaritTemplateFailsBeforeSqlWithTaskLocalCritiqueCategory() {
        run.setGabaritTemplate(null);

        EngineException error = assertThrows(EngineException.class, () -> strategy.process(task));

        assertEquals(RunError.CRITIQUE, error.getCategory());
        verifyNoInteractions(queryPort, structures);
    }

    @Test
    void zeroRowsSkipTemplateProjectButStillCreateCleanupPageAndAnchorMoves() {
        when(queryPort.executeQuery(eq(task.getSql()), anyMap())).thenReturn(List.of());

        strategy.process(task);

        verifyNoInteractions(structures);
        assertInstanceOf(BlocPage.class, task.getBlocsModify().get("REPORT_P0"));
        assertTrue(task.getBlocsModify().containsKey("REPORT_START"));
        assertTrue(task.getBlocsModify().containsKey("REPORT_END"));
    }

    @Test
    void absentRequiredColumnFollowsSafeDynamicSqlFailureContract() {
        Map<String, Object> incomplete = Map.of(
                "ID_GROUPE", 1,
                "ID_LIGNE", 1,
                "NOM_BLOC", "B",
                "VALEUR_BLOC", "V");
        when(queryPort.executeQuery(eq(task.getSql()), anyMap())).thenReturn(List.of(incomplete));
        when(queryPort.existColumn(eq("ID_GROUPE"), eq(List.of(incomplete)))).thenReturn(true);
        when(queryPort.existColumn(eq("ID_LIGNE"), eq(List.of(incomplete)))).thenReturn(true);
        when(queryPort.existColumn(eq("NOM_BLOC"), eq(List.of(incomplete)))).thenReturn(true);
        when(queryPort.existColumn(eq("VALEUR_BLOC"), eq(List.of(incomplete)))).thenReturn(true);

        EngineException error = assertThrows(EngineException.class, () -> strategy.process(task));

        assertEquals(RunError.CRITIQUE, error.getCategory());
        assertEquals("Unable to execute Dynamic SQL for task 40", error.getMessage());
        assertTrue(!error.getMessage().contains(task.getSql()));
        verify(structures, never()).ensureProject(eq(run), eq(run.getGabaritTemplate()));
    }

    @Test
    void rawSqlFailureIsWrappedWithoutCopyingSqlOrDriverMessage() {
        when(queryPort.executeQuery(eq(task.getSql()), anyMap()))
                .thenThrow(new IllegalStateException("SELECT dynamic data; secret bind"));

        EngineException error = assertThrows(EngineException.class, () -> strategy.process(task));

        assertEquals("Unable to execute Dynamic SQL for task 40", error.getMessage());
        assertTrue(!error.getMessage().contains("secret"));
        verifyNoInteractions(structures);
    }

    private DBlocInfo anchor(String uid, String name, int page, String left, String top,
                             String right, String bottom) {
        DBlocInfo anchor = new DBlocInfo();
        anchor.setUid(uid);
        anchor.setName(name);
        anchor.setPage(page);
        anchor.setLeft(new BigDecimal(left));
        anchor.setTop(new BigDecimal(top));
        anchor.setRight(new BigDecimal(right));
        anchor.setBottom(new BigDecimal(bottom));
        return anchor;
    }
}
~~~

### [86] `src/test/java/com/socgen/sgs/api/quark/engine/service/task/impl/QxpPreviousTaskProcessStrategyTest.java`

SHA-256: `4fcdbff84fd923268b139d48f24b00a2f0697bf60a85ecce53981d102414ce1b`

~~~java
package com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.service.task.impl;

import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.TestRuns;

import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.business.DocumentStructureBusiness;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.DocumentDomain;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.Run;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.RunProperties;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.bloc.BlocBox;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.project.QxpProject;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.task.TaskQxpPrevious;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.xml.QxpXml;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import java.util.List;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.mock;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.when;

class QxpPreviousTaskProcessStrategyTest {

    private DocumentStructureBusiness structureBusiness;
    private QxpPreviousTaskProcessStrategy strategy;
    private Run run;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        structureBusiness = mock(DocumentStructureBusiness.class);
        strategy = new QxpPreviousTaskProcessStrategy(structureBusiness);
        run = TestRuns.run();
        run.setId(42);
        run.setRunProperties(new RunProperties());
    }

    @Test
    void whitespaceSourceAndDestinationSelectExplicitModeAndRemainLiteral() {
        QxpXml xml = mock(QxpXml.class);
        when(xml.getValue(" ")).thenReturn("value");
        TaskQxpPrevious task = task(xml);
        task.setSourceBlocName(" ");
        task.setDestinationBlocName(" ");

        strategy.process(task);

        assertEquals(List.of(" "), List.copyOf(task.getBlocsUpdate().keySet()));
        assertEquals("value", ((BlocBox) task.getBlocsUpdate().get(" ")).getValue());
    }

    @Test
    void shorterDestinationRecordsCountErrorThenFailsAtOuterBoundary() {
        TaskQxpPrevious task = task(mock(QxpXml.class));
        task.setSourceBlocName("A|B");
        task.setDestinationBlocName("X");

        assertThrows(ArrayIndexOutOfBoundsException.class, () -> strategy.process(task));
        assertEquals(1, run.getErrors().size());
        assertEquals(1, run.getErrors().get(0).getCategory());
    }

    @Test
    void additionalDestinationsAreIgnoredAfterCountError() {
        QxpXml xml = mock(QxpXml.class);
        when(xml.getValue("A")).thenReturn("value");
        TaskQxpPrevious task = task(xml);
        task.setSourceBlocName("A");
        task.setDestinationBlocName("X|Y");

        strategy.process(task);

        assertEquals(List.of("X"), List.copyOf(task.getBlocsUpdate().keySet()));
        assertEquals(1, run.getErrors().size());
    }

    @Test
    void destinationPositionSuffixIsSeparatedWithoutTrimming() {
        QxpXml xml = mock(QxpXml.class);
        when(xml.getValue("A")).thenReturn("value");
        TaskQxpPrevious task = task(xml);
        task.setSourceBlocName("A");
        task.setDestinationBlocName(" X ¤ POSITION ");

        strategy.process(task);

        assertEquals(" POSITION ", task.getPositionBlocName());
        assertEquals(List.of(" X "), List.copyOf(task.getBlocsUpdate().keySet()));
    }

    @Test
    void hierarchyPreservesWhitespaceButReplacesExactEmptyValue() {
        QxpXml xml = mock(QxpXml.class);
        when(xml.getListBoxNameEndWith(".N0")).thenReturn(List.of("WHITE.N0", "EMPTY.N0"));
        when(xml.getListBoxNameEndWith(".N1")).thenReturn(List.of());
        when(xml.getValue("WHITE.N0")).thenReturn("   ");
        when(xml.getValue("EMPTY.N0")).thenReturn("");
        TaskQxpPrevious task = task(xml);
        task.setNullString("-");

        strategy.process(task);

        assertEquals("   ", ((BlocBox) task.getBlocsUpdate().get("WHITE.N1")).getValue());
        assertEquals("-", ((BlocBox) task.getBlocsUpdate().get("EMPTY.N1")).getValue());
    }

    @Test
    void missingStyleSourceIsNonBlockingUnspecifiedError() {
        QxpProject project = mock(QxpProject.class);
        when(project.getElements()).thenReturn(new java.util.LinkedHashMap<>());
        DocumentDomain source = new DocumentDomain();
        source.setQxpProject(project);
        TaskQxpPrevious task = new TaskQxpPrevious(100, run);
        task.setDocument(source);
        task.setSourceBlocName("MISSING");
        task.setDestinationBlocName("DEST");
        task.setConserverStyle(true);

        strategy.process(task);

        assertEquals(1, run.getErrors().size());
        assertEquals(1, run.getErrors().get(0).getCategory());
        assertEquals(0, task.getBlocsModify().size());
    }

    private TaskQxpPrevious task(QxpXml xml) {
        DocumentDomain source = new DocumentDomain();
        source.setQxpXml(xml);
        TaskQxpPrevious task = new TaskQxpPrevious(100, run);
        task.setDocument(source);
        return task;
    }
}
~~~

### [87] `src/test/java/com/socgen/sgs/api/quark/engine/service/task/impl/SystemTaskProcessStrategyTest.java`

SHA-256: `76e1761d53807d8a8ea7815d394de9a884a7bc85de33887ebb82700da4359ed3`

~~~java
package com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.service.task.impl;

import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.TestRuns;

import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.config.EngineFormattingProperties;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.Run;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.bloc.BlocBox;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.helper.DataTypeHelper;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.domain.task.TaskSystem;
import com.socgen.sgs.api.quark.engine.enums.DataTypeEnum;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;

class SystemTaskProcessStrategyTest {

    @Test
    void valueModeUsesTheSharedConfiguredFormatter() {
        EngineFormattingProperties properties = new EngineFormattingProperties();
        properties.setDatePattern("dd/MM/yyyy");
        properties.setDateTimePattern("dd/MM/yyyy HH:mm:ss");
        properties.setDecimalSeparator(",");
        properties.setGroupSeparator("_");
        properties.setCurrencySymbol("€");
        SystemTaskProcessStrategy strategy = new SystemTaskProcessStrategy(new DataTypeHelper(properties));
        TaskSystem task = new TaskSystem(1, TestRuns.run());
        task.setDestinationBlocName("TOTAL");
        task.setValue("1234.5");
        task.setDataType(DataTypeEnum.DECIMAL);
        task.setNbDecimal(2);
        task.setDecimalSignificative(true);
        task.setShowZero(true);

        strategy.process(task);

        assertEquals("1_234,50", ((BlocBox) task.getBlocsUpdate().get("TOTAL")).getValue());
    }
}
~~~
